// Generated by v1v2.pl 1.0 from halberd.xmi. Edit the result, not this tool output, once it is committed.
package HalberdInterceptorStructure {
    doc /* Converted from SysML v1 package 'Interceptor Structure' by v1v2.pl. */
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    private import ISQ::*;
    private import SI::*;
    private import SecMeta::*;
    private import V1Provenance::*;
    @Marking { level = Level::U; }
    @V1Source { xmiId = "_p_struct"; v1Name = "Interceptor Structure"; }

    port def TelemetryIF {
        @V1Source { xmiId = "_ib_tlm"; v1Name = "Telemetry IF"; }
        out attribute frameCount : Integer;
        in item status : StatusWord;
    }

    part def StatusWord {
        @V1Source { xmiId = "_b_status"; v1Name = "Status Word"; }
    }

    part def Vehicle {
        @V1Source { xmiId = "_b_vehicle"; v1Name = "Vehicle"; }
        attribute mass : MassValue;
    }

    part def Interceptor :> Vehicle {
        doc /* The Halberd interceptor. Notional. */
        @V1Source { xmiId = "_b_int"; v1Name = "Interceptor"; }
        attribute :>> mass : MassValue default = 42.5 [kg];
        part seeker : Seeker;
        part killVehicle : KillVehicle[1..2];
        attribute maxRange : HalberdUnitsLibrary::Range default = 300 [m];
        attribute mode : HalberdUnitsLibrary::SeekerMode default = HalberdUnitsLibrary::SeekerMode::search;
        attribute frame_ : DurationValue default = 0.01 [s];
        attribute navFilter; // v1 type: Navigation.mdzip#_19_0_3_ab12
        ref part launcher : HalberdGround::Launcher;
        port tlmOut : TelemetryIF;
        // v1 uml:StateMachine 'Flight Modes' not converted
        connect seeker.video to killVehicle.videoIn;
    }

    part def Seeker {
        @V1Source { xmiId = "_b_seeker"; v1Name = "Seeker"; }
        port video : TelemetryIF;
        port power; // v1 type 'Status Word' is a block, not an interface block
    }

    part def KillVehicle {
        @V1Source { xmiId = "_b_kv"; v1Name = "Kill Vehicle"; }
        port videoIn : ~TelemetryIF;
    }

    constraint def MassBudget {
        @V1Source { xmiId = "_cb_mass"; v1Name = "Mass Budget"; }
        // v1 constraint: {total <= limit} (convert by hand)
        in total : MassValue;
        in limit : MassValue;
    }

    // v1 uml:Activity 'Intercept' not converted
}

--- ./model/configurations/HalberdModelSatisfaction.sysml
// Generated by v1v2.pl 1.0 from halberd.xmi. Edit the result, not this tool output, once it is committed.
package HalberdModelSatisfaction {
    doc /* Satisfy relationships converted from SysML v1 by v1v2.pl. One part usage per
         * satisfying block, so that each satisfy has a feature to name. */
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    private import ISQ::*;
    private import SI::*;
    private import SecMeta::*;
    private import V1Provenance::*;
    @Marking { level = Level::U; }

    part interceptor : HalberdInterceptorStructure::Interceptor;
    satisfy HalberdInterceptorRequirements::interceptorSpecification.massWithinAllocation by interceptor;
    satisfy HalberdInterceptorRequirements::interceptorSpecification.seekerFrameTimeNotExceed by interceptor.seeker;

    part launcher : HalberdGround::Launcher;
    satisfy HalberdInterceptorRequirements::interceptorSpecification.range by launcher;
}

package HalberdModelVerification {
    doc /* Verification cases converted from SysML v1 verify relationships by v1v2.pl. The v1
         * test behavior is not converted; each case holds only its objective. */
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    private import ISQ::*;
    private import SI::*;
    private import SecMeta::*;
    private import V1Provenance::*;
    @Marking { level = Level::U; }

    verification def MassPropertiesInspection {
        doc /* v1 verifier: 'Interceptor Requirements::Mass Properties Inspection'
             * (uml:Activity). */
        @V1Source { xmiId = "_act_test"; v1Name = "Mass Properties Inspection"; }
        objective {
            verify HalberdInterceptorRequirements::interceptorSpecification.massWithinAllocation;
            verify HalberdInterceptorRequirements::interceptorSpecification.seekerFrameTimeNotExceed;
        }
    }
}

// Generated by v1v2.pl 1.0 from halberd.xmi. Edit the result, not this tool output, once it is committed.
package HalberdInterceptorRequirements {
    doc /* Converted from SysML v1 package 'Interceptor Requirements' by v1v2.pl. */
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    private import ISQ::*;
    private import SI::*;
    private import SecMeta::*;
    private import V1Provenance::*;
    @Marking { level = Level::U; }
    @V1Source { xmiId = "_p_req"; v1Name = "Interceptor Requirements"; }

    requirement <'HAL-INT'> interceptorSpecification {
        @V1Source { xmiId = "_r_spec"; v1Name = "Interceptor Specification"; }
        requirement <'HAL-INT-001'> massWithinAllocation {
            doc /* The interceptor mass shall not exceed its allocated mass. */
            @V1Source { xmiId = "_r_1"; v1Name = "Mass Within Allocation"; }
        }
        requirement <'HAL-INT-002'> seekerFrameTimeNotExceed {
            doc /* The seeker frame time shall not exceed 10 ms. */
            @V1Source { xmiId = "_r_2"; v1Name = ""; }
        }
        requirement <'HAL-INT-002'> range {
            doc /* Engagement range shall be at least 300 km. */
            @V1Source { xmiId = "_r_3"; v1Name = "Range"; }
        }
    }

    requirement <'N-001'> defendTheAsset {
        doc /* Defend the asset. */
        @V1Source { xmiId = "_r_need"; v1Name = "Defend the Asset"; }
    }

    // v1 uml:Activity 'Mass Properties Inspection': its verify relationships became a verification def
}

// Generated by v1v2.pl 1.0 from halberd.xmi. Edit the result, not this tool output, once it is committed.
package HalberdInterceptorStructure {
    doc /* Converted from SysML v1 package 'Interceptor Structure' by v1v2.pl. */
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    private import ISQ::*;
    private import SI::*;

    port def TelemetryIF {
        out attribute frameCount : Integer;
        in item status : StatusWord;
    }

    part def StatusWord;

    part def Vehicle {
        attribute mass : MassValue;
    }

    part def Interceptor :> Vehicle {
        doc /* The Halberd interceptor. Notional. */
        attribute :>> mass : MassValue default = 42.5 [kg];
        part seeker : Seeker;
        part killVehicle : KillVehicle[1..2];
        attribute maxRange : HalberdInterceptorStructureSliceStubs::Range default = 300 [m];
        attribute mode : HalberdInterceptorStructureSliceStubs::SeekerMode default = HalberdInterceptorStructureSliceStubs::SeekerMode::search;
        attribute frame_ : DurationValue default = 0.01 [s];
        attribute navFilter : Real;
        ref part launcher : HalberdInterceptorStructureSliceStubs::Launcher;
        port tlmOut : TelemetryIF;
        // v1 uml:StateMachine 'Flight Modes' not converted
        connect seeker.video to killVehicle.videoIn;
    }

    part def Seeker {
        port video : TelemetryIF;
        port power; // v1 type 'Status Word' is a block, not an interface block
    }

    part def KillVehicle {
        port videoIn : ~TelemetryIF;
    }

    constraint def MassBudget {
        // v1 constraint: {total <= limit} (convert by hand)
        in total : MassValue;
        in limit : MassValue;
    }

    // v1 uml:Activity 'Intercept' not converted
}

package ModelSatisfaction {
    doc /* Satisfy relationships converted from SysML v1 by v1v2.pl. One part usage per
         * satisfying block, so that each satisfy has a feature to name. */
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    private import ISQ::*;
    private import SI::*;

    satisfy InterceptorRequirements::interceptorSpecification.massWithinAllocation by interceptor;
    satisfy InterceptorRequirements::interceptorSpecification.seekerFrameTimeNotExceed by interceptor.seeker;

    part launcher : Ground::Launcher;

}

--- ./model/library/DoorsSource.sysml
// Generated by doors2v2.pl from SRS.csv, STP.csv, SYS.txt, dng.reqif. Re-run after each DOORS export and review the diff.
library package DoorsSource {
    doc /* Where an element came from in DOORS: module, object number and type, and any carried
         * attributes. Generated by doors2v2.pl. */
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    private import SecMeta::*;
    @Marking { level = Level::U; }

    metadata def DoorsObject {
        doc /* The DOORS object this element was imported from. */
        attribute module : String;
        attribute objectNumber : String;
        attribute objectType : String;
    }
    metadata def DoorsAttribute {
        doc /* A DOORS attribute value carried over as is. */
        attribute name : String;
        attribute value : String;
    }
}

package HalberdSRS {

            @DoorsAttribute { name = "Priority"; value = "Medium"; }
        }

                doc /* The interceptor shall meet its reliability allocation after ten years in
                     * storage. */
                @DoorsObject { module = "SRS"; objectNumber = "2.1-1"; objectType = "Requirement"; }
                @DoorsAttribute { name = "Priority"; value = "Low"; }
            }
        }

            @DoorsObject { module = "SRS"; objectNumber = "2-3"; objectType = "Requirement"; }
            @DoorsAttribute { name = "Priority"; value = "High"; }
        }
    }
    comment /* [SRS-8] 3 - Glossary */
    comment /* [SRS-9] 3-1 - Kill vehicle: the part of the interceptor that hits the target. */
}
